- REQ-404 WHEN `ask` is set, THE terminal client SHALL open over the field a box with the tool, what it is about to do (the command, the plan or the diff with its lines tinted) and the options as numbered rows; `1`, `2`, `3`, `y`, `a`, `n` or `↑` `↓` and `↵` SHALL answer (on the row «Otra…» of a question they focus its field instead, REQ-744), and no other key SHALL reach the field, except those of REQ-745.
- REQ-405 THE terminal client SHALL write where an answer came from on the tool's line once it is answered («permitido desde el móvil», «permitido por modo accept edits»).
- REQ-406 WHEN `/rename` is typed or the title is clicked, THE terminal client SHALL open a field for the name, and WHEN `/color` is typed a list of the six colours; the accent SHALL colour the pane's border and title and the `❯` marks.
- REQ-407 WHEN `^V` is pressed, THE terminal client SHALL read an image from the clipboard of the machine it runs on with the machine's own tools, put `[Imagen#n]` in the line and, before it is sent, make it no larger than 1568 px on its long side and a JPEG; IF the clipboard holds no image, THEN it SHALL say so on the status bar.
- REQ-408 WHEN `@` and some letters end the line, THE terminal client SHALL list the files of the workspace that hold them (`find_files`), and `↵` or `tab` SHALL put the path in the line.
- REQ-409 WHEN a line starts with `!`, THE terminal client SHALL send it with `conversation_shell` and draw its `shell` item as the shell's blocks are drawn.
- REQ-410 WHEN an item is clicked, THE terminal client SHALL select it (a bar lights it); `c` SHALL copy its text, `t` the whole turn and `esc` let go; the copy SHALL be OSC 52 and the status bar SHALL say how much was copied.
- REQ-411 THE terminal client SHALL scroll the conversation with the wheel and `PgUp` and `PgDn`, keeping the newest at the bottom. Over the text rows of the composer, when the text has more rows than its window, the wheel moves the window and not the list (REQ-707); so does it over those of a shell pane's field, and REQ-68 holds elsewhere.
- REQ-412 THE terminal client's sidebar row, the pane's title and Home SHALL mark `permission` with `!`, `question` with `?` and `agent_done` with `✓`, from the attention the daemon raised.
- REQ-413 WHILE `status` is `dead` or `resuming`, THE terminal client SHALL say «retomando conversación…» in place of the working line once a message is sent, and draw the restart's `divider`.
- REQ-414 WHEN `/exit` is sent, THE daemon SHALL close the conversation and THE pane SHALL be the shell again with a block for `claude` that names the conversation and how to resume it.
- REQ-415 THE terminal client SHALL draw every colour of the conversation it chooses from the theme in force.
- REQ-416 WHEN the line is `/` and letters with no space, THE terminal client SHALL open over the line the `commands` of the node whose names hold them (the ones that begin with them first), each with its description and its source, `↑` `↓` SHALL move, `tab` complete, `↵` run and `esc` close.
- REQ-417 (again after a reload: REQ-756) THE daemon SHALL fill `commands` from the engine's own answer to `initialize` (the name and the description of every command and skill), tagged `project`, `skill`, `plugin` or `claude` by where each is defined (the names `init` lists as skills tell skills from commands), and add jwn's own.
- REQ-418 THE daemon SHALL handle `/exit`, `/plan` (plan mode, and what follows it sent as the first message), `/rename`, `/color`, `/model`, `/effort` and `/mode` itself when they arrive in `conversation_send`, so that every client and `prompt` behave the same.
- REQ-419 WHEN `/cost` arrives, THE daemon SHALL answer with the conversation's own cost (the sum of its turns' `total_cost_usd`) and its model in an `out` item, and not send it to the engine.
- REQ-420 WHEN `/context`, `/usage`, `/recap`, `/goal`, `/mcp`, `/config` or `/agents` arrives, THE daemon SHALL send it to the engine and add its answer as an `out` item.
- REQ-421 WHEN `/compact` arrives, THE daemon SHALL send it, set `status` to `compacting` and, when the engine reports the boundary, add a `divider` of kind `compact` with the tokens before and after; WHEN `/clear` arrives it SHALL empty the items and take the new session's id.
- REQ-422 WHEN a command or skill of the project, of the person or of a plugin, or `/init`, `/review` or `/security-review`, arrives, THE daemon SHALL send it as typed, and the clients SHALL show the command and not its expansion.
- REQ-423 IF the engine answers a command that it «isn't available in this environment», OR the command needs Claude's own screen (`login`, `logout`, `vim`, `theme`, `terminal-setup`, `ide`, `chrome`, `doctor`, `insights`, `upgrade`, `keybindings`, `release-notes`), THEN the client SHALL say so and point at `command claude`, and the commands of the second kind SHALL be marked `screen` in `commands` and not sent.
- REQ-424 WHEN `/help`, `/status`, `/resume`, `/tasks`, `/artifacts`, `/skills`, `/permissions` or `/memory` is typed, THE terminal client SHALL open its own list (rows with `↑` `↓` `↵` `esc`) and send nothing to the engine.
- REQ-425 THE lists SHALL hold: for help, the keys and jwn's commands; for status, the model, effort, mode, session, folder, the engine's version and the limits; for resume, the conversations of this folder; for skills, the `commands` that are not Claude's; for permissions, the rules of the person's settings (read only) and those of this session; for memory, the person's `CLAUDE.md` and the project's `AGENTS.md`, which `↵` opens in an editor in a new pane.
- REQ-426 WHEN `list_conversations` arrives, THE daemon SHALL list the conversations Claude saved for the workspace's folder (id, name or first message, last change), newest first, up to 50.
- REQ-427 WHEN a conversation is chosen in the list of `/resume`, THE client SHALL close the current one and open the chosen with `open_conversation {resume}` in the same pane.
- REQ-428 THE node's `tasks` SHALL hold what the engine runs in the background (a subagent, a shell, a monitor, a scheduled loop), added when the engine says it started and set to `done`, `stopped` or `failed` when it ends, each with its description.
- REQ-429 WHEN the engine uses the `Agent` tool, THE daemon SHALL add an `agent` item (the agent's name and description) and, as the engine reports it, set its tools, tokens, time and last tool at most every 500 ms and add a step for each tool the subagent used (its name and argument); WHEN it ends it SHALL keep the totals the engine gives.
- REQ-430 THE terminal client SHALL draw an `agent` as `● Agent(name) description` with, while it works, `⠹ last tool · n herramientas · tokens · time` and, when it ends, the totals; its steps SHALL show as a list when opened (REQ-399).
- REQ-431 WHEN a task's event comes from the engine as a notification, THE daemon SHALL show it as a `note` and not as the markup the engine wrote.
- REQ-432 WHEN `conversation_stop_task` arrives, THE daemon SHALL ask the engine to stop that task; IF the engine cannot, THEN it SHALL reply an error that says so and change nothing.
- REQ-433 WHILE a task runs, THE terminal client's footer SHALL show `⚙ n`, and WHEN it is clicked, `^B` is pressed or `/tasks` is typed it SHALL list the tasks newest first, each with what it is and its time, and `x` SHALL stop the row it is on.
- REQ-434 WHEN the engine writes its checklist, THE daemon SHALL keep it in `todos` and THE terminal client SHALL draw over the conversation a strip, `☐ 2/4 tareas`, that `^T` or a click opens.
- REQ-435 WHEN the engine asks the person to choose, THE daemon SHALL set an `ask` of kind `question`, raise `question`, with its options (and `multi` when several may be chosen) and answer the engine with what was chosen or written (REQ-750), and the clients SHALL draw it as REQ-404 does with the title «pregunta».
- REQ-436 WHEN the engine asks to approve a plan, THE daemon SHALL set an `ask` of kind `plan`, raise `plan`, with the plan, whose answers SHALL be to approve and edit without asking (the mode becomes accept edits), to approve and ask for each edit (manual) and to go on planning.
- REQ-437 WHEN the engine publishes a page, THE daemon SHALL ask permission as for any tool and add the link to the `tool` item (`url`) and to `artifacts`, and the clients SHALL draw `↗ abrir en el navegador` under it and open it in the browser when chosen.
- REQ-438 WHEN the engine uses `PushNotification`, THE daemon SHALL add its text as a `note` and raise `agent_done`.
- REQ-439 WHILE no client is connected, THE daemon SHALL keep the engine, its tasks and its attention, so that what was started goes on and waits.
- REQ-440 WHILE a pane has a `conversation`, THE phone SHALL open its node and draw it flat in place of the pane's blocks, with the order and the marks of REQ-398 and REQ-400 and its text selectable.
- REQ-441 WHEN `ask` is set, THE phone SHALL open a sheet from the bottom with what the tool is about to do and a button of at least 48 px for each option, the first one primary, and for a question the row of REQ-752; a button SHALL send `conversation_answer`, and WHEN another client answers first the sheet SHALL go.
- REQ-442 THE phone's line at the foot SHALL hold only the field and send; the row above it SHALL hold at its left the pill of the model and the mode (`● sonnet-5.5 · manual`) and the branch, and at its right the `+`, in the column of the send button.
- REQ-443 WHILE `status` is not `idle`, THE phone's send button SHALL be the red stop, and a message sent SHALL be queued.
- REQ-444 WHEN the pill or the tab is tapped, THE phone SHALL open a sheet with the name, the six colours, the model, the effort and the mode, and send each change with `conversation_set` at once.
- REQ-445 WHEN `+` is tapped, THE phone SHALL list a file of the workspace, a photo, an image of the gallery, the commands and a shell line; typing `/`, `@` or `!` first SHALL do the same.
- REQ-446 WHEN `/` and letters are typed, THE phone SHALL list the `commands` above the keyboard, and a tap SHALL run the command.
- REQ-447 WHEN an image is attached, THE phone SHALL make it no larger than 1568 px on its long side and a JPEG, show it above the line as a pill with its size and a ✕, and send it with the message, at most four in a message and each no larger than 5 MiB.
- REQ-448 WHILE a task runs, THE phone's header SHALL show a pill `⚙ n` that opens the tasks as a sheet with a `■` on each that can be stopped, and its checklist SHALL be a bar under the tab that a tap opens.
- REQ-449 WHEN an item is tapped, THE phone SHALL select it and offer over the line *Copiar*, *Copiar el turno* and *Soltar*, and say how much was copied.
- REQ-450 THE phone SHALL draw the lists of `/help`, `/status`, `/resume`, `/tasks`, `/artifacts`, `/skills` and `/permissions` as sheets.
- REQ-451 THE phone's tab, Home's rows and Avisos SHALL mark `permission`, `question` and `agent_done` as REQ-412 does, and REQ-36 stands: no approve or deny control on Home or Avisos, only inside the conversation.
- REQ-452 THE phone SHALL draw every colour of the conversation from its theme, and the conversation's accent from the same six.
- REQ-453 WHEN `prompt` has a target pane with a `conversation`, THE binary SHALL send `conversation_send` and not wait for the screen to settle; IF it has none, THEN it SHALL do as before (REQ-363).
- REQ-454 WITH `prompt --wait`, THE binary SHALL wait for the turn to end (at most 10 minutes, or `--timeout`) and print the text of the final answer; IF a question or a permission waits, THEN it SHALL stop waiting, print what it asks and exit 3.
- REQ-455 WHEN `read` has a target pane with a `conversation`, THE binary SHALL print its last `--lines` items as text, as REQ-395's version does.
- REQ-456 WHEN `worktree --task` finds the conversation of the new worktree, THE binary SHALL send the text with `conversation_send`.
- REQ-457 THE skill the binary carries SHALL say that `prompt` reaches the agent as a message, what `--wait` returns and that a question can be waiting.
- REQ-458 WHEN an engine starts, THE daemon SHALL send it `initialize` and, from its answer, set `commands` (REQ-417) and, while `model` is still the placeholder or the name it was asked with (`opus`), set it to the model the engine says that is, so that the model is shown before any turn.
- REQ-459 WHEN the line starts with a command that is in `commands` (or with `!`), THE terminal client SHALL write that first word in the colour of where the command is defined (jwn blue, Claude magenta, the project green, a skill yellow, a plugin cyan; `!` green) and bold, and a word that is no command SHALL stay plain.
- REQ-460 THE `commands` of a conversation SHALL hold every command of Claude Code's own screen (jwn's list is made from its commands page), also before the engine has answered: the ones the engine serves are the engine's, tagged by where they are defined, and the rest are `screen`, which the client draws dimmed and the daemon refuses with the way to `command claude`; a command of the project or a plugin with the same name SHALL replace the listed one.
- REQ-461 WHEN the left button is pressed on the list of messages of a conversation in the terminal client and no list or field is open over it, IT SHALL begin a drag and let go of what was selected before; IF the pointer never moves, THEN the press SHALL be the click it always was (picking an item).
- REQ-462 WHILE a drag is held in a conversation, THE terminal client SHALL select from where it began to the row and column under the pointer, and past the top or the bottom of the list scroll it a line every 60 ms and select to its first or last row.
- REQ-463 WHEN the button is let go after the pointer moved, THE terminal client SHALL copy what is selected through OSC 52, as it reads: without the mark that begins each row (`❯`, `●`, `⎿`), the rows the width broke in one line, the blank line between items kept, and say «copiado · N líneas · M caracteres».
- REQ-464 THE selection of a conversation SHALL be lit as REQ-308 says and stay lit until the next press or key, and the wheel SHALL not let go of it.
- REQ-465 WHEN `read_markdown` arrives, THE daemon SHALL reply with the blocks of that file of the workspace (`path` relative to the workspace's folder, or absolute) and its title and path.
- REQ-466 IF the file is outside the workspace's folder (what a link leads to counted), is not a Markdown file (`.md`, `.markdown`), is not UTF-8 text or weighs more than 1 MiB, THEN THE daemon SHALL refuse it, say why, and read nothing else; the phone asks through the same command.
- REQ-467 WHEN `read_plan` arrives for the plan that waits in a conversation, THE daemon SHALL reply with the whole plan as blocks (at most 64 KiB, `truncated` when it was longer) and the plan's file when the engine said it; IF that plan is not waiting, THEN it SHALL refuse.
- REQ-468 THE blocks SHALL be headings (1 to 6), paragraphs, quotes, lists (numbered or not, with task marks), code with its language, tables and rules, and their text plain, bold, italic, code or a link; what Markdown has beyond them SHALL be text, never lost.
- REQ-469 WHEN `v` is pressed with a plan waiting in the terminal client's conversation, IT SHALL open the plan in the viewer; the first lines in the box stay.
- REQ-470 WHEN a `.md` file of the tree is opened (`↵` or a click), THE terminal client SHALL open it in the viewer; any other file SHALL open in the editor as before.
- REQ-471 THE viewer SHALL cover the area of the panes and draw the blocks: headings in three colours, lists whose wrapped rows hang under their text, `☐` and `☑` for tasks, quotes with a bar, tables with borders, code on its own ground with its language and the tones of its syntax, code in a line, bold, italic and links underlined; a bar above with the name, the kind and how far it is read, and one below with the keys. (Amended by N167: the viewer is a panel of the layout, not a cover of it, REQ-728 and REQ-731 to REQ-733.)
- REQ-472 WHILE the viewer is open, `j` `k` `↑` `↓` SHALL move a line, `␣` and `PgDn`, `b` and `PgUp` a page, `g` `G` to the ends, `[` `]` to the previous and next heading, and the wheel three lines.
- REQ-473 WHEN `/` is typed in the viewer, IT SHALL read a word and light every match, `↵` SHALL keep it and `n` `N` SHALL go to the next and the previous match; `esc` while typing SHALL cancel it.
- REQ-474 WHERE the panel is 100 columns wide or more, `o` SHALL show or hide the headings at its left, with the one being read lit.
- REQ-475 WHILE the viewer shows a plan that waits, `1` `2` `3` SHALL answer it as the box does and close the viewer; IF it was answered elsewhere, THEN it SHALL say so and keep the document.
- REQ-476 WHEN `y` is pressed in the viewer, IT SHALL copy the document as text through OSC 52 and say how much.
- REQ-477 WHEN `esc` is pressed in the viewer, IT SHALL close it and leave things as they were. (`e` no longer opens the document's file in an editor: REQ-316.)
- REQ-478 WHEN a drag is made over the viewer, IT SHALL cover rows and columns, copy what it reads like and stay lit as REQ-461 to REQ-464 say of the conversation.
- REQ-479 WHEN a `.md` of the phone's file list is tapped, THE phone SHALL open it in the viewer; any other file opens as text (REQ-618).
- REQ-480 WHILE a plan waits, THE phone's question sheet SHALL offer «Ver el plan completo», which opens the plan in the viewer with the three answers under it.
- REQ-481 THE phone's viewer SHALL be a whole screen with a bar (back, the title, `☰` listing the headings as a sheet, a tap scrolling to one) and the document drawn from its blocks in the theme's colours.
- REQ-482 THE daemon SHALL find the settings of a repository in this order: its `.jw.toml`, then the personal file (REQ-483), then the defaults; the first that exists is the only one read.
- REQ-483 THE personal file SHALL be `<config dir>/projects/<key>.toml`, where `<key>` is made of the repository's `origin` (lowercase, without scheme, user or `.git`, with `/` as `_`) or, when it has none, of its path (`local_` and the path with `/` as `_`); a worktree and the repository it belongs to SHALL have the same one.
- REQ-484 THE personal file SHALL have the syntax of `.jw.toml` and an error in either SHALL name the file it is in and the line.
- REQ-485 THE reply of `project_config` SHALL say which file it read (`from`: `repo` or `personal`, none when it read neither) and the path of the personal file, whether it exists or not.
- REQ-486 WHEN `write_project_config {repo, to, text}` arrives, THE daemon SHALL check the text as it checks a file and only then write it, whole and at once, to the personal file (`to: personal`, readable by its owner only) or to the repository's `.jw.toml` (`to: repo`); IF the text is not valid, THEN it SHALL refuse it, say why and the line, and write nothing.
- REQ-487 THE daemon SHALL never create or change a repository's `.jw.toml` unless asked with `to: repo`, and WHEN it writes the personal file of a repository that has its own `.jw.toml`, it SHALL say that the personal one is hidden by it (`shadowed`).
- REQ-488 WHEN a repository has neither file, THE terminal client's box and the phone's sheet SHALL say that it opens with the defaults and ask nothing; WHEN the settings are the personal file's they SHALL say «según tu configuración».
- REQ-489 THE settings SHALL have `copy`, a list of paths relative to the repository, and `project_config` SHALL list them; IF a path is empty, absolute or goes up with `..`, THEN the file SHALL be refused, naming the path and the line.
- REQ-490 WHEN a worktree is made, THE daemon SHALL copy each file of `copy` from the repository's checkout to the same place in the worktree, making the folders it needs and giving the copy only the permission bits of its owner.
- REQ-491 THE daemon SHALL skip a file, and say why, that is not in the checkout, is a link or not a regular file, resolves outside the checkout, weighs more than 1 MiB or is already in the worktree; it SHALL never copy over a file or write outside the worktree.
- REQ-492 A file that cannot be copied SHALL NOT make the worktree fail: the reply of `create_worktree` SHALL list what was `copied` and what was `skipped` with its reason.
- REQ-493 WHEN `jwn worktree` makes a worktree that copied or skipped files, IT SHALL say so on its error output and keep its output as it was.
- REQ-494 THE settings SHALL have `ports`, a table of names (lowercase letters, digits and `_`) to offsets from 0 to 99, and `project_config` SHALL list them; IF a name or an offset is not valid, THEN the file SHALL be refused naming it and the line.
- REQ-495 WHEN a worktree is made of a project that has `ports`, THE daemon SHALL give it the lowest **slot** no other workspace holds and, with it, a block of 100 ports starting at 20000 + 100 × slot, keep both with the workspace (saved with the session) and release them when the workspace is removed.
- REQ-496 THE daemon SHALL start every pane of a workspace that has ports with `<PREFIX>_PORT_BASE`, the first port of its block, and one `<PREFIX>_PORT_<NAME>` (the name in capitals) for each name: the base and the name's offset.
- REQ-497 THE reply of `create_worktree` SHALL list the ports it gave (`ports`: name to port); IF no block is left below 65536, THEN it SHALL refuse before it makes anything.
- REQ-498 A change of `ports` in the settings SHALL NOT change the ports a worktree already has: it applies to the next one.
- REQ-499 WHEN `jwn worktree` makes a worktree that got ports, IT SHALL say them on its error output and keep its output as it was.
- REQ-500 WHEN `start_workspace` is asked for a folder (a workspace that is not a worktree), THE daemon SHALL read the folder's settings (REQ-501) and start the panes of their `[layout]` with their agent and editor as it does for a worktree, and nothing else: no setup, no ports, no copies.
- REQ-501 THE settings of a folder SHALL be those of its repository (REQ-482) when the folder is the top level of a git repository, and otherwise its own `.jw.toml` if it has one, else the personal file named by its path (`local_` and the path, REQ-483), else none: a folder inside a repository is not the repository.
- REQ-502 IF the settings of a folder have no layout, THEN `start_workspace` SHALL start one shell; IF they cannot be used, THEN it SHALL refuse naming the file and the line, and start nothing.
- REQ-503 WHEN a folder is opened, THE terminal client and the phone SHALL ask for `start_workspace` and, if it fails, say why and open one shell.
- REQ-504 THE settings SHALL have `open`, `shell` or `claude` (`shell` when it is not said): the one panel a workspace is opened with; IF it is anything else, THEN the file SHALL be refused naming the file and the line.
- REQ-505 THE daemon SHALL ignore `[layout]`, `[agent]` and `editor`: a file that has them reads as one that has not, so the files of N78 and of the old jw work as they are.
- REQ-506 WHEN `start_workspace` arrives for a workspace with no panes, THE daemon SHALL start one pane, a shell or `claude` (started as `new_pane` starts it, REQ-338) as `open` says, and reply its id; WHERE the command carries `open`, THAT SHALL be used in place of the settings'.
- REQ-507 WHERE the settings of a worktree have `setup` lines, THE daemon SHALL run them in that one pane first, one after the other, writing `❯ ` and the line before each and stopping at the first that fails, with `{name}` and `{branch}` replaced by their values quoted for the shell; WHEN they end well it SHALL leave the shell or start `claude`, and IF one fails, THEN the pane SHALL stay a shell with what was written on its screen and `claude` SHALL NOT start.
- REQ-508 WHEN the lines of a setup end, THE pane SHALL raise `shell_done` with the exit status of the line that failed, or 0, by reporting it the way a shell integration does.
- REQ-509 THE reply of `project_config` SHALL have `open` in place of `panes`.
- REQ-510 THE terminal client's box and the phone's sheet SHALL say what the worktree opens with (`shell` or `claude`, and that its setup runs first when it has one) and let the person change it for this worktree, with `tab` or a chip; the client SHALL then send `start_workspace` with that choice.
- REQ-511 WHEN `jwn worktree` has `--task`, THE worktree SHALL be opened with `claude`; `--open shell` or `--open claude` SHALL choose it otherwise.
- REQ-512 WHEN a worktree is made from a name the person gave (the box, the phone's sheet, `jwn worktree <name>`), THE client SHALL send that name as the branch, as written, and its last `/` part as the folder; `jwn worktree --branch` SHALL give another branch and keep the name's last part as the folder.
- REQ-513 THE box and the sheet SHALL accept as a name parts of lowercase letters, digits and hyphens joined by `/`, no part empty, none starting with a hyphen or over 40 characters, the whole at most 60; IF it is not one, THEN they SHALL say so and make nothing.
- REQ-514 THE settings SHALL have no template of branches: `project_config` SHALL NOT reply a `branch`, and a `branch` key in a file SHALL be ignored, whatever it holds.
- REQ-516 THE reply of `project_config` SHALL carry `root_text`, the `root` key as the file wrote it, when the file has one.
- REQ-517 WHEN `edit_project_config` arrives, THE daemon SHALL read the file `to` says (an absent one is empty), put in it each key of `set` whose value is not what the file says, take out the key of an `root`, `setup`, `copy` or `ports` that is empty, write it as REQ-486 says and reply `{path, shadowed}`; IF the file or the result is not valid by REQ-333, THEN it SHALL change nothing and name the file and the line.
- REQ-518 WHEN `edit_project_config` writes a file, THE daemon SHALL keep what `set` does not touch as it was (comments, order, other keys and tables), keep the comment after a value it replaces, and put a new key at the top level.
- REQ-519 WHERE a field of `set` equals what the file says, THE daemon SHALL not touch its key, so that a `set` with nothing different leaves the file as it was byte for byte.
- REQ-520 WHEN `p` is pressed on a workspace of a repository, THE terminal client SHALL open the project screen with the file the settings come from (the repository's, the personal one, or none) and the fields `root`, `setup`, `copy`, `puertos` and `al abrir`; IF the workspace is a folder that is no repository, THEN it SHALL say so and open nothing.
- REQ-521 WHILE the project screen is open, THE terminal client SHALL give it the keys: `↑ ↓` choose a field, `↵` edit it (a text, a line of a list, or the choice of shell or claude), `a` add and `d` remove a line of a list, `g` choose the file saved to, `s` save, `esc` close.
- REQ-522 WHEN `esc` is pressed with changes not saved, THE screen SHALL say so and close on a second `esc`; WHEN another key comes between them, THE screen SHALL cancel the warning.
- REQ-523 WHEN a port is typed, THE screen SHALL accept `name=offset` with a name of lowercase letters, digits and `_`, not `base` and not repeated, and an offset from 0 to 99; IF it is not one, THEN it SHALL say why and keep the line as it is typed.
- REQ-524 WHEN saving, THE client SHALL send the fields that changed, and every field when the file saved to is not the one the settings came from, and nothing when nothing changed and the file is that one.
- REQ-525 WHEN the daemon answers a save, THE client SHALL say where it went and, when `shadowed`, that the repository's own file hides it, or say why it did not, and SHALL read the settings again.
- REQ-526 THE phone SHALL offer «configuración del proyecto» in the sheet of a repository's workspace and of a worktree, and show the same fields, the choice of file and the save, with every touch target at least 48 dp.
- REQ-527 WHILE the program in a pane's terminal is on the alternate screen, THE pane's `screen` SHALL carry `spans`: exactly `rows` rows, each the row's cells as `Span` (as in the `blocks` node: text, `fg`, `bg`, `b`, `i`, `u`) with neighbouring cells of one style joined, the colours as the terminal has them (a palette index or `#rrggbb`, none for the default) and the blank cells at the end of a row left out unless they carry a background or an underline.
- REQ-528 WHEN a cell is drawn reversed, THE daemon SHALL put its colours swapped in the span, a default colour becoming index 0 as text and index 7 as ground.
- REQ-529 WHILE the program is not on the alternate screen, THE pane's `screen` SHALL have no `spans`, and `lines` SHALL be there in every case.
- REQ-530 WHEN the terminal client draws a pane's screen that has `spans`, IT SHALL draw each span with its colours: indexes 0 to 15 as the theme's, as in the blocks, the rest of the 256 as always, an RGB colour as it is asked and no colour as the theme's own text and ground; WHEN it has none, IT SHALL draw `lines` in the theme's text colour as before.
- REQ-531 THE daemon SHALL still not answer a program's questions about its colours (RAT-25, OPEN-12) after REQ-527.
- REQ-532 WHEN the phone draws a pane's screen that has `spans`, IT SHALL draw each span with its colours (the sixteen named as the phone's theme, the rest of the 256 and an RGB colour as asked, no colour as the screen's own text and ground, bold, italic and underline) and the cursor's cell as a block; WHEN it has none, IT SHALL draw `lines` as before.
- REQ-533 WHEN `list_worktrees` arrives, THE daemon SHALL reply with the worktrees `git worktree list` gives for the repository `repo` is in, from the repository or from one of its worktrees, except the main worktree (a bare repository's own folder is that one), each as a `WorktreeInfo`.
- REQ-534 WHEN `open_worktree` arrives with a worktree `list_worktrees` lists, THE daemon SHALL add a workspace of kind `worktree` for it, named `repo: branch` (`repo: (commit)` for a detached head), with the main worktree as its `project`, its branch and no panel, give it a block of ports as REQ-495 says WHERE the project's settings name ports, and make, copy and run nothing.
- REQ-535 IF `open_worktree` names a path that `list_worktrees` does not list (the main worktree among them), whose folder is not there, or that a workspace has open, THEN THE daemon SHALL refuse with `not_found`, `not_found` or `conflict`, the paths compared as the disk spells them, and IF `repo` is not in a repository, THEN with `git_failed`.
- REQ-536 WHEN `start_workspace` starts the panel of a workspace made by `open_worktree`, THE daemon SHALL NOT run the project's setup, and SHALL start the panel as REQ-506 says in every other way.
- REQ-537 THE box of `w` in the terminal client SHALL have two sides, `nuevo` and `existentes`, that `←` and `→` change, the first being the one shown, and WHEN the second is shown for the first time THE box SHALL ask `list_worktrees` for the repository and then say `existentes N`; WHILE the second is shown, THE box SHALL draw a row for each worktree with its branch (its commit when detached), whether it is open, has changes, has no branch or has no folder, and its folder.
- REQ-538 WHILE the side of the worktrees that exist is shown, `↵` SHALL open the marked ones, else the highlighted one, `espacio` SHALL mark or unmark the highlighted one that can be opened, `a` SHALL open all that can be opened and `esc` SHALL close the box, a worktree that is open or whose folder is not there SHALL be neither marked nor opened and `↵` on it SHALL say why, and after opening THE box SHALL stay, say how many were opened or why one was not and list the worktrees again.
- REQ-539 THE phone's worktree sheet SHALL have two sides, `nuevo` and `existentes`, as pills of at least 48 dp, and the sheet of a repository's workspace and of a worktree SHALL offer «worktrees existentes» (WHERE its repository is known), which opens it on the second; WHEN the second is shown for the first time, THE sheet SHALL ask `list_worktrees` and show a row for each worktree with its branch (its commit when detached), its folder and whether it is open, has changes, has no branch or has no folder, with `abrir` on the ones that can be opened and «abrir los N cerrados»; WHEN one or more are opened THE sheet SHALL stay, say what it did as the terminal client does (REQ-538), say that the workspace has no panel until the person goes in, and list the worktrees again.
- REQ-540 WHEN `open_conversation` resumes a session that jwn has not saved for that pane (`resume`, `-r <id>`, `-c`, the `/resume` list), THE daemon SHALL read the engine's transcript of it (`<config dir>/projects/<folder>/<session>.jsonl`, as REQ-546 says) and make the node's items from it, in order: the person's messages, Claude's text, its tools with their state, a line that says what they answered and the first lines of their output (a tool with no result as `failed`, «sin resultado»), and its compactions as dividers, and then the `resume` divider.
- REQ-541 THE items of REQ-540 SHALL NOT include what Claude Code wrote itself (reminders, the wrapper of a command, of its output, of a shell line and of a background task's notice, a note that a turn was interrupted, text with the model `<synthetic>`; a message of the person that merely starts with `<` is kept), Claude's thinking, the checklist and the tool search, or a line of a subagent.
- REQ-542 WHEN that transcript says a mode, a model or an effort, THE node SHALL show the last `permission-mode`, the model of Claude's last message and the effort of its last one, and WHEN the engine starts for the next message THE daemon SHALL start it with `--permission-mode`, `--model` and `--effort` of them, unless the person typed others; an effort that `--effort` does not take SHALL NOT be passed, and a mode jwn does not have (`bypassPermissions`, `dontAsk`) SHALL NOT be restored.
- REQ-543 IF the transcript is not there, cannot be read, or the session id could name a file outside the folder of transcripts (a `/`, a `\` or a `.`), THEN THE daemon SHALL resume as it did before, with the `resume` divider only.
- REQ-544 WHEN the daemon saves a conversation whose engine has written its transcript and that was not saved with its own items before N101, THE saved file SHALL hold the items that are jwn's own (notes, the answers of local commands, shell lines, the card of a subagent, the end of a turn with its time and cost, and the dividers of a restart, a clear and a resume), each with the time it was made at, and none of the person's messages, Claude's text or tools; WHEN it opens one, THE daemon SHALL make the items from the transcript and put the saved ones among them, each after the last line made no later than it, a subagent's card taking the place of the tool that started it.
- REQ-545 WHILE a conversation made from a transcript has no engine, THE daemon SHALL look at the transcript every second and SHALL show the whole lines that were added, with the mode, the model and the effort they say, WHEN what the lines say changed (a line that makes no item is no change), and SHALL stop looking when the conversation's own engine starts.
- REQ-546 THE daemon SHALL read the end of a transcript, as far back as it takes for the conversation to hold the items a node keeps (4, then 16, then 64 MiB, or the whole file when it is shorter), and WHILE it follows one SHALL read only what was added; a line that does not end in what is read SHALL be read further until it does, a line longer than 64 MiB SHALL be skipped to its end, and a transcript that becomes shorter SHALL be read again from its end.
- REQ-547 WHEN `<bin> claude` runs in a pane of the daemon, in a terminal, with `-r` or `--resume` and no session id (alone, or with a search term that is not one), THE binary SHALL list the conversations saved for the folder it is in, those whose name has the term in it when there is one, in the pane's own terminal, newest first, and WHEN one is chosen it SHALL send `open_conversation` with that session as `--resume`, in place of the flag, with the rest of the arguments.
- REQ-548 WHILE the list of REQ-547 is shown, `↑` `↓` `j` `k` `g` `G` and `Home` `End` SHALL move the chosen row, `↵` SHALL choose it, and `esc`, `q` and `^C` SHALL leave and end the binary with status 0 without opening anything; IF the folder has no conversation (or none with the term), THEN it SHALL say so and end the same way.
- REQ-549 WHILE a conversation has messages that wait for the turn that runs (`queue`), THE terminal client SHALL draw each after the last item as a message not sent yet (`⧖` and the text, dim), THE phone SHALL draw the first three the same way and say how many more there are, and WHILE a conversation is followed from a transcript THE daemon SHALL put in its `queue` the messages the other `claude` has typed and not taken in yet (its `enqueue` lines, until a `dequeue` or a `remove` of the same text, or the line of the person's that carries it), leaving out what Claude Code queues itself (a task's notice).
- REQ-550 WHEN a transcript has a `thinking` block of Claude that says something and is no longer than 1,200 characters (the summary of its thinking, which Claude Code shows between its tools), THE daemon SHALL make a `text` item of it, in its place among the lines; a block with no text (the thinking is redacted) or a longer one SHALL NOT be shown.
- REQ-551 WHEN a transcript has an `attachment` of type `queued_command` whose `prompt` is the person's (the message typed while a turn ran and taken in during it), THE daemon SHALL make a `user` item of it, in its place, and it SHALL stop waiting (REQ-549), without it beginning a turn.
- REQ-552 WHEN a transcript has a `system` line `turn_duration`, THE daemon SHALL make a `result` item of it with its duration, the tools used since the person's last message and no cost, and the clients SHALL leave the cost out of the line that ends a turn when it is 0.
- REQ-553 THE daemon SHALL NOT make an item of a `user` line of a transcript that Claude Code wrote when it compacted the conversation (`isCompactSummary` or `isVisibleInTranscriptOnly`); the `compact_boundary` line is its divider.
- REQ-554 WHEN a transcript has an error of the API (a line with `isApiErrorMessage`) THE daemon SHALL make a `note` of it with the tone `error`, WHEN a message of the person was interrupted (`[Request interrupted…]`) a `note` «Interrumpido» with the tone `warn`, and text of the model `<synthetic>` that is not an error («No response requested.») SHALL NOT be shown.
- REQ-555 WHEN a transcript has the notice of a background task (`<task-notification>`) THE daemon SHALL make a `note` of its `summary`; WHEN a command of Claude's own (`<command-name>`) a `user` item with the command and its arguments, and WHEN what it printed (`<local-command-stdout>`, or a `system` line `local_command`) an `out` item with its lines, without the colour codes; WHEN a `system` line `informational` a `note` with its text.
- REQ-556 WHEN a transcript has a tool of Claude's that edits or writes a file, THE `tool` item SHALL carry the diff that REQ-379 gives, made from the arguments of the tool; a `queued_command` whose `prompt` is made of blocks (a message with a picture) SHALL be read as REQ-551 says, with its pictures counted.
- REQ-557 THE daemon SHALL NOT make an item, from a line of a transcript written by jwn's own engine (`entrypoint` `sdk-cli`), of an interruption, a command and what it printed, an informational line, the end of a turn or an error of the API, because jwn keeps its own items for them (REQ-544); the person's messages, Claude's text and its tools are read from every line.
- REQ-558 WHILE a conversation has tools of the classes `Bash`, `Grep` and `Glob`, `Read` and `LS` that have ended (`ok`, `denied` or `failed`) one after the other, THE terminal client SHALL draw them as one row for each run, `▸` and what they did in words, the classes in the order search, read, list and shell, the first with a capital and the plural when there is more than one (`Buscó 1 patrón, ejecutó 3 comandos`), also for a run of one tool; WHEN a click is on that row, it SHALL open the run (`▾`, and the tools one by one as before) or close it and SHALL pick nothing and drop a pick that the closed run hides, WHEN `^O` is for a tool that is folded it SHALL open its run, and the arrows SHALL skip the items that have no row.
- REQ-559 A tool of another class (an edit, a write, a subagent, a skill), a tool that runs or waits for an answer, and anything that is not a tool SHALL end a run and be drawn by itself as before, and THE phone SHALL draw the runs the same way, with a row of at least 48 dp, and open them by a tap.
- REQ-560 WHEN a text of Claude is finished (the engine's complete message, or a line of its transcript) and holds more than plain paragraphs (a table, a list, a quote, a code block, a heading, a rule, or text in bold, italic, code or a link), THE daemon SHALL parse it once with the Markdown reader of the viewer and send the blocks in the item's `blocks` (additive, REQ-465 to REQ-468), a line break inside a paragraph staying a line break as Claude wrote it and not the space the viewer makes of it; a text that is plain paragraphs, one that is still arriving, one longer than 64 KiB and one whose quotes and lists nest deeper than 32 levels SHALL carry none and be drawn from `text` as before. The blocks SHALL NOT be saved with the item and SHALL be made again from `text` when the file is read.
- REQ-561 THE terminal client and the phone SHALL draw the `blocks` of a text as the viewer draws them (REQ-471, REQ-481) under the `●` of the message, with the text selectable and, in the terminal, a click on a row picking the item as before; the quote's bar and a list's hang are only how it is set and a copy leaves them out, while a table is copied as drawn (`c` still copies Claude's own text). A link is drawn underlined in blue as in the viewer, with no address after it.
- REQ-562 WHILE a tool runs, THE node SHALL list it in `running` with the id of its item and when it began, and THE terminal client and the phone SHALL draw it open (what it does and `ejecutando…`) with how long it has run counted from that time, `● Bash(cargo test) · 8 s`; when it ends, it leaves the list and is drawn and folded as any ended tool (REQ-558). The daemon takes the time from when the item was made.
- REQ-563 WHILE a turn runs in a conversation that jwn drives, THE node SHALL say in `tokens` what Claude has written in it (each message of the engine counted once, by its id, with the most it says; the count starts again with each turn), and THE working line SHALL say it after the time, `Pensando… (12 s · ↓ 1,3 k tokens · esc interrumpe)`; with none said, the line is as before.
- REQ-564 WHEN a conversation is resumed by its id, THE daemon SHALL show «conversación retomada» before the first message sent in it and only once, and SHALL NOT show it while the session is only followed; it is lost if the daemon restarts before that message.
- REQ-565 WHILE the transcript that a conversation follows has a turn open (it began with a message of the person and no line has ended it: `turn_duration`, or an interruption) and the last line is not older than 15 minutes, and a `claude` that is busy has the session open in Claude's registry of processes (with none, or one that only waits for a message, the turn was cut off and nothing runs it: no line of work, the user, 2026-10-19: «dice pensando cuando realmente no está pensando»), THE node SHALL say in `following` when the turn began (the time of that message), and THE terminal client and the phone SHALL draw the line of work, `Pensando… (11 min 34 s · ↓ 28,7 k tokens)`, or `Trabajando…` while a tool runs, with no `esc interrumpe` and no way to stop it, because the conversation has no engine of its own for it. `status` and `since` do not change, so nothing that interrupts or queues is offered.
- REQ-566 WHILE such a turn is open, THE tools of it that have no result yet SHALL be `run` (a turn begins with the message of the person, or with the first line of Claude's after the line that ended the last one when no message of the person's began it: a notice of a task, a message of another agent) and listed in `running` with the time of their line, and drawn as REQ-562 says; WHEN the line that ends the turn is read, an interruption, a compaction or an error of the API is read, the person writes again (a command of Claude's own, `/clear` or `/model`, does not begin a turn) or the turn is left behind (no line, also of a subagent, for 15 minutes), the tools that have none SHALL be `failed` with «sin resultado». THE tokens of the turn SHALL be what the `usage.output_tokens` of Claude's lines add up to, each message once by its id.
- REQ-567 WHEN a conversation has a transcript that may hold items older than its node's, THE node SHALL say in `older` the time of its first item that is of the transcript (an item of jwn's own that is older than it does not say where the window is in the file), and `read_older {node, before, count}` SHALL answer with up to `count` (at most 500) of the items made before `before`, oldest first, read from the transcript with the items of jwn's own that were made in that time among them, and `older`, the time to ask with for the ones before them. `older` SHALL always be earlier than `before` and left out when the file holds no more, so a client that asks again with it comes to the start. A tool of the page SHALL be answered by a result a little past the cursor, and one no line answers SHALL be taken as interrupted. A conversation with no transcript to read answers `not_found`.
- REQ-568 WHEN the list of a conversation is scrolled to its top and the node says `older`, THE terminal client SHALL ask for 100 items, one request at a time, draw what comes before the others with the view where it was, and keep up to 3,000 of them; the row above the first item SHALL say that there are more (`▲ hay anteriores · sube para cargar`), that they are loading, that the file could not be read (a click asks again), where the conversation starts or that the rest is left in Claude's file. WHILE older items are held and the node's window slides, THE items that leave its head SHALL go to the end of the older ones, so the two stay in a row; a window that does not join them (another epoch, a `first` that went back or past what the window held) SHALL drop them. What is held by the number of an item (the one picked, the tools opened, the runs unfolded) SHALL stay with the item, and a selection of rows SHALL be let go when older items come.
- REQ-569 WHEN the list of a conversation on the phone reaches its oldest end (it cannot scroll further back) and the node says `older`, THE phone SHALL ask for 100 items, one request at a time, hold them beside the node's window and draw what comes before the others with the list where it was; the row above the first item SHALL say what REQ-568 says, a tap on the failed one asking again. WHILE older items are held and the window slides, THE items that leave its head SHALL go to the end of the older ones, and a window that does not join them (another epoch, a `first` that went back or past what the window held) SHALL drop them. The number of an item (what is picked, opened or unfolded) SHALL not change when older items come, and the rows SHALL be keyed by it, so the list does not jump.
- REQ-570 WHEN Claude consults the advisor (a `server_tool_use` named `advisor` in its message, and its `advisor_tool_result` in a later line), THE conversation SHALL show a row as it shows a tool: `● Advising`, running until the answer comes, and under it `✔ El asesor revisó la conversación y aplicará sus comentarios` (the user, 2026-10-18, with a capture of Claude Code: «esto falta … no se ve cuando se realiza un advising»); an answer that is an error says that the advisor could not answer, and a consultation that no line answers when the turn ends was interrupted. The row is not folded with other tools. It does not say which model the advisor is: the transcript does not.
- REQ-571 A message that another agent sent to the session (the report of a subagent, `<agent-message …>`, or the line «Another Claude session sent a message:» that precedes it) SHALL NOT be shown: neither as a message of the person's, among the items, nor as one that waits, in the queue. What the person pastes of such a report into their own message is theirs and is shown (the user, 2026-10-18: «más texto que no debería de aparecer por defecto»; it showed as `⧖` lines and as messages of the person's, since N103).
- REQ-572 THE daemon SHALL start the engine of a conversation with `--prompt-suggestions true`, and WHEN the engine sends a `prompt_suggestion` after a turn THE node SHALL carry it in `suggestion` (trimmed, at most 400 characters; an empty one is none) while the engine is stopped and nothing runs, asks or waits in the queue, and SHALL drop it when a turn or a local command begins or the engine goes. The suggestion SHALL NOT be saved, and a session that is only followed has none (the transcript does not keep it). WHERE `config.toml` says `suggestions = false`, `jwn claude` SHALL add `--prompt-suggestions false` to the engine's arguments (unless the person gave one of their own), and the daemon SHALL then not add its own, so the engine is given exactly one and the call is not made; a file with no such key, or that cannot be read, leaves it on.
- REQ-573 WHILE the node has a suggestion and the field is empty and nothing runs, asks, is open over the field or was put aside, THE terminal client SHALL draw it grey in the field followed by `tab`, `tab` or `→` SHALL put it in the field to be edited or sent, `esc` SHALL put it aside, and `enter` with the field empty SHALL send nothing; THE phone SHALL draw it as a row of 48 dp over the field with a ✕, a tap on it putting it in the field and the ✕ putting it aside. Writing in the field, or pasting a text or a picture into it, SHALL hide the suggestion while the field has text, and it SHALL show again when the field is empty again (after deleting what was written, or what it was taken as); only `esc` and the ✕ put it aside, and the same text SHALL not come back after that, while another one does (the user, 2026-10-18: «si escribo y luego borro desaparece lo cual está mal»).
- REQ-574 THE row of a consultation of the advisor SHALL say the model it runs on as Claude Code does, `● Advising using Opus 5.5`, taking `advisorModel` from the `.claude/settings.local.json` of the conversation's folder, else its `.claude/settings.json`, else Claude's own `settings.json` (the user, 2026-10-18: «el nombre del modelo está en settings.json de .claude»); an alias (`opus`) is the newest model of that name among the keys of `modelSettings`, and just its name when none is listed. The settings are read when the conversation is opened, and the transcript, which does not say the model, is not changed; a conversation with no `advisorModel` has the row as before.
- REQ-575 WHILE a conversation has no engine of its own, THE daemon SHALL look, with the transcript, for another process of Claude that has the conversation's session open (the files `<claude folder>/sessions/<pid>.json` that Claude Code writes for each live process, whose `sessionId` is the session's, whose pid is alive and runs a `claude` that is not the engine this conversation started last), and THE node SHALL say it in `held` (its pid, its folder and whether it works or waits for a message), also when it only waits; WHEN a message is sent and such a process is alive, THE daemon SHALL refuse it with `conflict` naming the pid and the folder and SHALL NOT start the engine, and the node SHALL be free again within a second of that process ending.
- REQ-576 WHILE the node has `held`, THE terminal client and the phone SHALL say in the field who has the session («solo lectura · otro claude (pid 93269) la tiene abierta · ciérralo»; on the phone a row over the field with the folder, the field and the send button not usable) and SHALL NOT write, paste or send in it (what is typed says why in the status bar), and SHALL propose no suggestion; reading, scrolling, copying and loading older items SHALL go on (the user, 2026-10-18: «claude code es el harness principal, es mejor evitar tocar sobre el mismo»).
- REQ-577 IN the field of the conversation in the terminal client, `shift` `enter` SHALL write a line break, `option` `←` and `→` (and `esc b`, `esc f`, which many terminals send for them) SHALL move the cursor a word, `option` `shift` `←` and `→` SHALL select by words and `shift` `←`, `→`, `home` and `end` SHALL select by characters (the selection drawn on the field, writing or a deletion replacing it, a plain arrow going to its edge), and `option` `backspace` SHALL take the word before the cursor (or the selection). THE client SHALL ask the terminal for the protocol of keyboards that says the modifiers of every key (kitty's, `DISAMBIGUATE_ESCAPE_CODES`), when it answers that it speaks it, and give it back on leaving; without it `shift` `enter` arrives as `enter` and `^J` is the line break (the user, 2026-10-19: «shift enter no funciona … option arrow salta una palabra, option shift arrow selecciona la palabra y option backspace limpia la palabra»). The phone has its own keyboard and selection.
- REQ-578 WHEN a conversation that has an engine changes its mode (the person chose it, or Claude's approval of a plan did), THE daemon SHALL remember it in `last-mode` in its state folder, and WHEN a conversation is opened that is neither resumed nor given `--permission-mode`, it SHALL start in that mode (`manual` while none was remembered); a conversation that is resumed keeps its own (REQ-544), and one that only follows another `claude`'s transcript says nothing of it (the user, 2026-10-19: «Claude Code recuerde en nuevas sesiones el modo que tenía»).
- REQ-579 WHILE a conversation of a workspace thinks, writes, runs a tool or compacts (`status` is `thinking`, `streaming`, `tool` or `compacting`; waiting for an answer is a mark of its own), THE workspace summary SHALL say `working: true` (left out when not), and THE terminal client SHALL draw a spinner beside the marks of its row in the sidebar, at the right (the legend says «⠋ trabaja»), and THE phone SHALL draw a spinner beside the mark of its row and, when the row has no attention to say, the line «Claude trabaja». The marks do not change: a mark that is not seen is drawn as before, with the spinner next to it (the user, 2026-10-19: «no hay marca de trabajando»; of the two places that the prototype `docs/prototypes/working-mark.html` offered he chose the spinner beside the marks).
- REQ-580 THE terminal client SHALL leave a blank row between the list of a conversation and the line of work, and between a folded run of tools and the item that follows it (a tool that runs is not part of the run before it), so the rows do not touch (the user, 2026-10-19, with a capture: «se puede agregar espacio en el TUI entre estas líneas»).
- REQ-581 WHEN `list_prs` arrives, THE daemon SHALL read the open pull requests of the repository the workspace is in with `gh pr list --state open --limit 100` (and `--search review-requested:@me` when `requested`) and reply with them most recently updated first, each with its number, title, author, branches, size, review decision and changed files; IF `gh` is not installed THEN it SHALL refuse with `unsupported`, and IF `gh` fails (no login, no GitHub remote, no answer in 40 seconds) THEN with `git_failed` and gh's own words. It SHALL call `gh` only to read.
- REQ-582 WHEN `review_pr` arrives, THE daemon SHALL, with no lock held, read the pull request (`gh pr view`, and its comments on the code through `gh api`), fetch its head into `refs/<product name>/pr-<n>` and its base branch from `origin`, make the worktree `review-<n>` beside the project (in the folder the project's settings give worktrees) on the branch `review/pr-<n>` at that head, run the project's setup, and start the workspace's one panel with `claude`; IF a fetch or a read fails THEN it SHALL make nothing and say why. WHERE the review is already a workspace, it SHALL fetch again, move it to the new head with `reset --hard` unless the worktree has work of the person's (changes in the folder, or commits on top of the head it was last put at: then leave it as it is and reply `dirty`), and start its panel only when it has none; a worktree that is made and cannot be added as a workspace SHALL be taken away again, so that the next try does not find the folder.
- REQ-583 THE summary and the node of a workspace that reviews a pull request SHALL say `review: {pr, base}` (`base` the branch the pull request is to be merged into); the field SHALL be saved with the session, be left out of every other workspace, and be ignored by a client that does not know it.
- REQ-584 BEFORE the agent of a review starts, THE daemon SHALL write `reviews/<repository>-pr-<n>.md` in its state folder with the pull request's title, description, conversation, reviews, comments on the code by file and line, and files, cut at 200 KB with the cut said; the diff SHALL stay in git, and the file SHALL be written again each time the review is brought to a new head.
- REQ-585 THE conversation in a review workspace SHALL start in `manual` (`--permission-mode manual`, so it asks before it edits, whatever mode the person last worked in), SHALL NOT be briefed (nothing is sent to it), SHALL offer as its suggestion a first message that names the file of REQ-584 (`Lee <file> y dime los riesgos del PR #<n>`, taken as REQ-573 says), and its mode SHALL NOT be what a new conversation starts in (REQ-578).
- REQ-586 WHILE a workspace reviews a pull request, THE changes of its worktree (`general` and the list of commits) SHALL compare against `origin/<base>`, the base of the pull request, and not against the repository's default branch; AND a review branch with no commits beyond the head that was fetched SHALL count as having no unpushed work (`check_workspace` says `ahead: 0`).
- REQ-587 WHEN `R` is pressed after the leader at the workspace or panel level, THE terminal client SHALL open the list of the open pull requests of the project of the highlighted workspace and ask the daemon for them (`list_prs`) in a thread and a connection of its own; WHILE they are read it SHALL show a spinner row, and IF the daemon cannot (no `gh`, no login, no GitHub remote) THEN it SHALL close the list and say why in the status bar. THE list SHALL show each pull request's number, title, author, size and review state (`✓ aprobado`, `✗ cambios pedidos`, `● sin revisar`, `◌ borrador`), filter by what is typed (number, title, author, branch), move with `↑` and `↓`, and under the list show the selected one's branches, description and changed files; `m` with nothing typed SHALL ask again for only the pull requests that ask for the person's review (and again for all), `o` SHALL open its address in the browser (only `http` and `https`), and `esc` SHALL close it.
- REQ-588 WHEN `↵` picks a pull request, THE terminal client SHALL ask the daemon for its review (`review_pr`, in a thread and a connection of its own, up to five minutes), the list SHALL say what is being done and take no other key, a failure SHALL leave the list where it was and say why («no se hizo nada: …»), and a review that was made SHALL close the list and open the workspace as soon as the daemon shows it; a review that already existed SHALL say that the last version was brought, and one with changes of the person's that it was left as it was. A workspace that reviews a pull request SHALL say `⎇#<n>` beside its name in the sidebar.
- REQ-589 WHILE the open workspace reviews a pull request and the terminal has room for both (the area is at least 70 columns wider than the agent's 40, or two fifths of it), THE terminal client SHALL draw the workspace as `|agent|diff|`: its panels on the left and, on the right, the diff of the pull request against its base (the viewer of `d` as a pane, on its `General` view, asking for no other), with the file tree off; the diff SHALL be the panel at the right of the last one (`l` or `tab` reach it, `h` comes back), SHALL have the keys after `↵` when it is focused (and after `d` or a click in it), its border SHALL be lit while it has the focus, and the leader or `esc` SHALL give the keys back to the commands; `d` SHALL NOT open a second viewer. WHERE the area is narrower, `d` SHALL open the diff as a screen, as in every other workspace. The diff SHALL be let go (its nodes no longer read) when another workspace is the one shown. (Amended by N167: the diff is a view that the daemon opens beside the agent, REQ-726; the draw of `|agent|diff|` is the layout's.)
- REQ-590 WHEN `d` is pressed in the terminal client and the area has room for the diff beside the panels (REQ-589), THE client SHALL open the diff of the highlighted workspace at the right of its panels, on the uncommitted changes, and give it the keys; WHILE it is open, `d` SHALL close it (in a review, where it stays, it gives the keys back), `z` SHALL make it fill the area and `z` again give the panels their width back, and every other key SHALL be the viewer's own (`j` `k` files, `[` `]` hunks, `1` `2` `3` the views, `v` seen, `s` columns, `r`, `esc`); the diff SHALL stay open when another workspace is highlighted, showing that one's, and SHALL be let go when it is closed. WHERE there is no room, `d` SHALL open the diff as a screen over the panels, as before (the user, 2026-10-19: the diff is not to be full size, and 1 2 3 keep changing its view). (Amended by N167: `d` toggles the diff view and does not give it the keys, `z` is retired for `f`, and the diff does not follow the highlighted workspace, REQ-728, REQ-729 and REQ-736.)
- REQ-591 WHILE the diff is drawn in one column, THE viewer SHALL wrap a line that is longer than the body: the rest of it goes on in the rows that follow, in the line's colour, with `↪` where the numbers are and none of its own, the changed words following the characters they are in; `w` SHALL turn the wrapping off (a long line is cut, as before) and on again, it SHALL be on when the viewer opens, and the rows that wrapping makes SHALL be rows to scroll. In two columns a line is cut (the user, 2026-10-19: «si hace overflow el texto que lo pase a la siguiente línea, como vscode y nvim»).
- REQ-592 WHEN `V` is pressed in the body of the diff viewer, THE viewer SHALL begin picking lines at the first row of code at the top of the body (`v` keeps marking the file as seen); WHILE lines are picked, `j` and `k` SHALL move the end of the pick by a row, inside the file it began in, the picked rows SHALL be lit, `esc` SHALL let go (and not close the viewer) and `↵` SHALL ask for where they are, as `@<path>#L<from>-<to>` over the lines of the new side that were added or are as they were, or `#L<from>-<to> (antes de los cambios)` when only deleted lines are picked, followed by a space; a pick SHALL be let go when the view or the document changes. WHEN the viewer is beside the panels, THE terminal client SHALL write that in the field of the conversation of the open workspace (the focused panel's, else the first), give the keyboard to it and send nothing; WHEN there is no conversation, or the viewer is the screen of `d`, THE client SHALL copy it (OSC 52) and say so (the user, 2026-10-19: «si selecciono unas líneas que se haga referencia para Claude, así sabe qué líneas de qué archivo estoy mencionando»). The mouse does not pick yet.
- REQ-593 WHEN an engine says its commands, THE daemon SHALL keep that list in `commands.json` in its state folder, and WHEN a conversation is opened whose engine has not started and that has no list of its own (one resumed by its id, not one restored from its saved file, which has its own), it SHALL start with the kept list and jwn's own, so `/` completes and colours a command or skill of Claude from the first key instead of after the first message (the user, 2026-10-19: «cuando escribo /something que sea un comando o skill de claude no me aparece en color ni me ayuda a autocompletar»).
- REQ-594 WHILE the daemon stops, THE shells that end SHALL NOT be taken as programs that ended: the panes stay in the session, and an agent that ran in one keeps what a restart brings back (REQ-391); a shell that ends at any other time still takes its pane with it and a program that ends still forgets its resume (the user, 2026-10-19: after a restart an agent of another worktree was no longer running claude; the one pane out of three that lost its `resume` had it cleared by the poll of the programs while the shells were being killed).
- REQ-595 WHEN «Seguir planeando» of a plan that waits is chosen (`3`, `n`, `↵` on it or a click in the terminal client, its tap in the phone's question sheet, and `3` in the plan viewer of either), THE client SHALL NOT answer at once but ask what to do next: the terminal turns the options into the line `▸ …` (`↵` sends, `esc` goes back to the options, an empty line is the plain refusal), the phone opens a text field under the option with «Volver» and a button that sends; the answer SHALL be `conversation_answer` with key `no` and the text written (none when empty), which the daemon says to Claude as «El usuario no aprobó el plan y dice: …». The «No» of a permission SHALL keep answering at once (the user, 2026-10-19: «el caso de seguir planeando vamos a cambiarlo para que nos deje agregar un texto con el cual podamos dar la siguiente instrucción rápida»). Prototype: `docs/prototypes/plan-reply.html`.
- REQ-596 WHEN a `/word` is being written (the cursor ends it, and it begins the line or follows a space or a new line; a word with a second `/` is a path and is not one), THE terminal client and the phone SHALL open the list of REQ-416 and REQ-446 for it wherever it is in the line, and a word of the line that is a command that exists SHALL be written in the colour of where it is defined (jwn blue, Claude magenta, project green, skill yellow, plugin cyan) wherever it is, also on the rows a long line goes on in. Choosing one (`Tab`, `↵`, a tap) SHALL run it only when the word is the whole line; anywhere else it SHALL write `/name ` in place of the word and send nothing, since in the middle of a message it is text for Claude (the user, 2026-10-19: «escribo /something … no me aparece en color ni me ayuda a autocompletar», «con todos los comandos en medio»). Prototype: `docs/prototypes/compose-and-plan-sheet.html`.
- REQ-597 WHILE a plan waits, THE phone's question sheet SHALL show only the beginning of the plan, formatted as the viewer draws it (the first blocks, cut at 150 dp with a fade; the first lines as they are until the PC has read the document), with «▤ Ver el plan completo» right under it and the three answers after, and the viewer SHALL open the document already read (the user, 2026-10-19: the sheet printed the plan as raw Markdown, 200 lines, with the button three screens down). Prototype: `docs/prototypes/compose-and-plan-sheet.html`.
- REQ-598 THE terminal client's footer SHALL say, from the left, what runs (`⚙ 1 agente · 2 shells · 1 monitor`, yellow, each kind only when one of it runs, `⚙ n` with the total when the pane is narrow, and nothing while nothing runs), the mode in its colour with `⇧tab` dim after it, and the model, and at the right edge `^/ teclas` (`^/` blue); a click on each SHALL open what it names (the tasks, the mode list, the model list, the keys). A pane too narrow for all of it SHALL give way from the right: the word `teclas`, then the hint `⇧tab`, then the words of what runs, which fall back to their total; the mode and the model are the last to go. THE keys `^/` (and `^?`, which a terminal cannot tell from it, read by the client as `^/`, `^7` or `^_`) and `?` with the field empty SHALL open the list of keys (the user, 2026-10-19: «agentes · modo · modelo y algún botón como ctrl + ? para los keybinds»). Prototype: `docs/prototypes/footer-keys.html`.
- REQ-599 THE list of keys SHALL be a list of the conversation's own (rows with `↑` `↓` `esc` and a filter typed in it), with each key, what it does and the group it is in (writing, Claude, what is seen, jwn); `/help` SHALL list the same keys before the commands.
- REQ-600 THE `agent` item SHALL carry the `prompt` the subagent was given (clipped to 600 characters; none when it is blank) and the `model` it runs with (what its own messages say, short: `haiku-5.5`), and the `task` SHALL carry `item`, the `id` of the card that shows it, so that a client puts them together; both are optional and additive (the user, 2026-10-19: «ver los subagentes que están corriendo y qué hacen»). The steps, the tools, the tokens and the time are the card's already (REQ-430), what a running task does now is its `info`, and when it ends its `info` is what it said (one line, 400 characters at most).
- REQ-601 WHERE a conversation of the terminal client has 100 columns or more inside its panel, `^B` and a click on `⚙` of the footer SHALL open or close, at the right of the conversation (46 columns, 60 from 150), the area of what runs, and open it WITH THE KEYS IN IT. It SHALL have the title `en marcha · n corriendo` (`nada` when nothing runs, keeping its place, and `nada corre ahora`; bold when it has the keys, dim when the field has them) and three sections in this order, each with a heading and its count and only when it has tasks: `subagentes`, `shells` (what Claude runs in the background with `Bash`) and `monitores` (`Monitor`, the loops and what the engine does not name), the newest first and the newest six of each. A subagent's line SHALL be `▸ ⠼ name (model) → what it does` with `time · tokens` at the right; a shell's, its command (the `arg` of the tool call `task.item` names, else the engine's description) with its time; a monitor's the same, with `◉` (`↻` for a loop) in place of the spinner; one that ended SHALL say `✓`, `✗` or `■`. Under the lists, for the one lit (the first that runs unless another was chosen), in sections: for a subagent what it is, `prompt`, `pasos` and `ahora` or `dijo`; for a shell `comando` and `ahora` or `terminó` (what the engine says it does, never the command again); for a monitor `vigila` (`repite` for a loop) and `último`. WHILE the area has the keys, `↑` `↓` `j` `k` SHALL walk every line across the sections, `←` `→` `h` `l` jump to the first line of the next section or, going back, of this one and then of the previous, `g` `G` (`Home` `End`) go to the ends, `x` stop the one that runs, `⇥` give the keys to the field leaving the area open (and back), and `esc` close it; any other key without `^` SHALL be ignored there and those with `^` (and the page keys) SHALL work as in the field; `^B` SHALL close it and give the keys back. A click on a line SHALL light it and give the keys to the area. WHERE the panel is narrower, `^B` SHALL list the tasks over the conversation as before, by section, each tagged with its kind (the user, 2026-10-19: «separar los monitors y los shells», «navegar con el teclado en la pantalla de ctrl + b»). Prototype: `docs/prototypes/background-area.html`.
- REQ-602 THE phone SHALL show, in the row of the field by the model and the branch, a chip `⚙ n` while anything runs (a subagent, a shell or a monitor; and while the panel is open, so it can be closed), a touch target of 48 dp, that opens or closes a panel under the bar of the tab, never taller than 300 dp: a small heading for each of the three sections (`subagentes`, `shells`, `monitores`, as REQ-601) and in each one row of at least 48 dp for each task, the newest six first, with its state, its name (`name (model)` and `→ what it does` for a subagent, the command for the others) and its numbers at the right; a tap SHALL open the row in place with what it is (as REQ-601 says of each kind) and, while it runs, a button of 48 dp that stops it. The pill `⚙ n` of the bar under the tab (REQ-448) is the chip now; `/tasks` still opens its sheet with every task.
- REQ-603 WHILE a question, a permission or a plan waits in the terminal client, `esc` and `^C` SHALL interrupt it as they interrupt a turn that runs (the daemon refuses the question that waits first, so the engine is not left asking), and the box that asks SHALL say `esc interrumpe`; WHILE the reason of «seguir planeando» or the field of «Otra…» (REQ-746) is written `esc` SHALL go back to the options, and from there stop (the user, 2026-10-19: «no hay forma de interrumpir una pregunta o un plan… por más que presiono esc no puedo cancelarla»). The phone already stops it with the red `■` of its field.
- REQ-604 WHEN `cancel` arrives for a workspace, THE daemon SHALL stop what runs for it that can be stopped (a `sync_workspace` push, a `review_pr` being made) by killing the process it waits on (`git` or `gh`) and what that started, and the operation SHALL answer with the error `cancelado`; IF nothing runs there, THEN `cancel` SHALL answer ok all the same, and WHILE one runs for a workspace a second SHALL be refused (`conflict`). A review stopped half way SHALL leave no worktree behind. WHILE the terminal client waits for the push (`subiendo…`) or for the review of a pull request (`trayendo la rama…`), `esc` SHALL send `cancel` once, the box SHALL say `cancelando…` until the answer, and the answer `cancelado` SHALL be said as a line (`subida de <rama> cancelada`, `no se hizo nada: cancelado`) and not explained as a failure (the user, 2026-10-19: «cuando hago el sync no tengo forma de cancelar, agrega esc para cancelar cualquier evento»). The other waits of the client already had `esc` (the changes being read, the list of pull requests, the folders being read, the project's settings, the phone link).
- REQ-605 THE daemon SHALL remember the mode the engine was in just before it said it is in `plan`, and WHEN a plan asks for approval and that mode was `auto`, THE first answer SHALL be «Aprobar y volver a auto» with the key `auto` (instead of «Aprobar y editar sin preguntar», `accept`), and answering it SHALL allow the plan and ask the engine for `auto` at once, as `accept` asks for `acceptEdits`; from any other mode, or when the conversation began in plan, the answers SHALL be as REQ-436 says. The three answers SHALL stay three, in the same places (`1` `2` `3` keep their meaning); a client draws the label and sends the key it is given. The mode remembered SHALL be forgotten when the engine reports any other mode, SHALL be the same whether the engine's own message or the line it wrote in its transcript says `plan` first, and SHALL be kept on disk while the conversation is in plan, so a restart does not lose it (the user, 2026-10-19: «cuando salgo de plan mode en vez de ser en auto sale en accept edits»). Prototype: `docs/prototypes/plan-modes.html`.
- REQ-606 WHILE the phone link is on, THE daemon SHALL read what `cloudflared` prints for as long as the tunnel lives (a program that writes to a pipe nobody reads is killed by SIGPIPE: the tunnel ended four minutes after it started, with the daemon still offering its host name), and SHALL look at the tunnel every 5 seconds: WHEN it has ended, THE daemon SHALL say so in its log, forget it, tell the connected phones that it is gone (`endpoints`) and open another, whose host name is new and is told to them as the first was (REQ-189); IF none could be opened it SHALL try again, and it SHALL stop looking when the link is turned off (the user, 2026-10-20: «el tunnel no está funcionando, tengo mi teléfono sin wifi y no se conecta»).
- REQ-607 WHILE a shell or a monitor of the engine runs, and once more after it ended, THE daemon SHALL read the last 16 KB of the file the engine writes its output to (named by the Bash result, by the notification's `output_file`, or found beside one of the same session by the session and the task id) outside its lock, every 2 seconds, and SHALL keep in the task's `tail` the last 6 lines of it, newest last, as plain text: no escape sequences or control characters, a carriage return keeping the last state of its line, each line cut at 200 characters, the blank ones at the ends dropped and, once the task ended, the engine's closing `[exited with code n]` line dropped; THE daemon SHALL send it only when it changed. IF the path is not absolute or has `..`, is a link or not a regular file, is not named `<task>.output` in a `tasks` directory inside the directory of the task's session, THEN THE daemon SHALL NOT read it.
- REQ-608 THE detail of a lit shell or monitor in the area of `^B` (REQ-601) SHALL end with `salida` and its `tail` (`sin salida todavía` when it has none), dim, the newest line last, and when the room is short the oldest lines SHALL go first; WHEN a shell or a monitor is opened in the phone's panel, it SHALL show the last 4 lines of the `tail` in a box of the theme's background colour, one line each, and a button of at least 48 dp that opens all of them, long lines wrapped; a subagent and a loop SHALL show no `salida`.
- REQ-609 WHEN the engine starts a task of type `local_bash` for a call of the `Monitor` tool, THE daemon SHALL make it a task of kind `monitor` (the engine gives a monitor the type of a shell), so that it is listed with the monitors.
- REQ-610 THE daemon SHALL answer `read_file {workspace, path}` with a `TextDoc` of a regular UTF-8 file inside the workspace's folder: its lines, each as runs `{t, k?}`, an empty line with no runs.
- REQ-611 IF the path resolves, once links are followed, outside the workspace's folder, or is not a file, or does not exist, THEN the daemon SHALL refuse with `bad_request` (outside, not a file) or `not_found` and read nothing, and SHALL read the handle whose type it checked.
- REQ-612 IF the file weighs more than 1 MiB, THEN the daemon SHALL read only its first MiB, cut after its last line end (at its last whole character when it has none), SHALL set `truncated` and SHALL give the file's weight as `size`.
- REQ-613 IF the first 8000 bytes of a file hold a NUL byte, or its text is not UTF-8, THEN the daemon SHALL refuse with the error code `binary`, and the client SHALL say so with the size it knows from the listing.
- REQ-614 THE daemon SHALL drop the `\r` of a CRLF and cut a line after 2000 characters with `…`; THE terminal client SHALL show a tab as 4 columns.
- REQ-615 WHEN `↵` or `l` is pressed on a file of the tree that is not Markdown, THE terminal client SHALL open it in the viewer; a `.md` SHALL still open rendered. `e` SHALL open nothing, in the tree or in a viewer (REQ-316).
- REQ-616 WHILE a text file is open, THE viewer SHALL show line numbers, scroll with the wheel, `␣` and `PgDn` and `PgUp` (a page) and `g` and `G` (the ends), search with `/` and `n` `N`, copy with `y`, toggle wrapping with `w`, reread with `r` and close with `esc` or `q`; the cursor, `h` `j` `k` `l`, `v` and `↵` are REQ-639; WHILE wrapping is off it SHALL mark a cut line with `›`.
- REQ-617 WHERE the file is a `.md`, THE terminal client's viewer SHALL switch between its rendered view and its source with `s`.
- REQ-618 WHEN a file is tapped in the phone's files, THE phone SHALL ask `read_file` and open the answer full-screen, in monospace with a number for each line (REQ-642), selectable by a long press, with a bar of Back, the name, how many lines and what it weighs; a file the PC cut SHALL say so under the text.
- REQ-619 IF `read_file` has no answer in 10 s, THEN the client SHALL say that the daemon does not answer (an old one may need updating) and stay on its list.
- REQ-620 THE viewer SHALL NOT change the file, and SHALL NOT refresh it by itself when it changes on disk.
- REQ-621 WHERE the file's extension, its name (`Makefile`, `Dockerfile`) or its `#!` line names a language of the grammar pack, THE daemon SHALL mark each run with one class of `kw str num ty fn cm attr`, or none; otherwise it SHALL send plain runs.
